$data_generation_residual
[1] "An in-place edit that changes no row count and no largest identifier of these tables, and a data change made while an R session is open, leave that session's generation unchanged. The fixed-synopsis components whose bounded contributions changed still redraw one by one (their noise is keyed on the realised contributions too), so no exact difference is released, but comparing answers from before and after such an edit can show which components changed (not by how much). Custodians who edit data in place can redraw every fixed synopsis by changing dsomop.dp.privacy_epoch."

[1] "An in-place edit that changes no row count and no largest identifier of these tables, and a data change made while an R session is open, leave that session's generation unchanged. $data_generation_residual
[1] "An in-place edit that changes no row count and no largest identifier of these tables, and a data change made while an R session is open, leave that session's generation unchanged. The fixed-synopsis components whose bounded contributions changed still redraw one by one (their noise is keyed on the realised contributions too), so no exact difference is released, but comparing answers from before and after such an edit can show which components changed (not by how much). Custodians who edit data in place can redraw every fixed synopsis by changing dsomop.dp.privacy_epoch."
